# Australian Renewable Energy Agency Glossary — Top Projects per Term

For each glossary term, the projects where it is most characteristic (project-share vs corpus base rate). Inverse of the per-project signatures.

**Method.** For each term with ≥5 corpus mentions, find projects with ≥2 mentions where the project-share is ≥3.0× the corpus base. Top 25 per term.

**Coverage.** 760 terms.

## Categories

| category | n terms |
|---|---:|
| technology | 231 |
| organisation | 186 |
| concept | 141 |
| market | 42 |
| location | 39 |
| regulation | 33 |
| programme | 29 |
| event | 25 |
| unit | 18 |
| standard | 11 |
| person | 5 |
